# EXECUTION_QUEUE.md — Project-level sequential execution queue

**Owner directive 2026-05-29 (Batch 432):** *"We have been jumping all over the place and the items get missed. Lets bring order to the chaos."*

**Owner directive 2026-05-29 (Batch 444, lifecycle clarification):** *"Resolved items need to go down in the queue after being tagged as resolved. If needs a new iteration, needs be tagged as reopened and to be moved to top of queue. Add findings to the queue document as well. Note that such items need to be added in the queue document at each turn! This is mandatory!!!"*

**Purpose.** Single ordered list of the next project-level execution items. The **top of the queue is the next thing Claude executes**. Items can be reordered, but whatever sits at the top still executes next. This is the project-level analogue of per-session todos — it survives across sessions.

**Distinction from todos.** TodoWrite tracks in-session task state for *one* conversation. This queue tracks *project-level* obligations across sessions. A todo is "what am I doing in this conversation"; a queue item is "what is the project's next milestone."

---

## Rules

1. **Top item is next execution.** When work is needed and the queue is non-empty, the top `PENDING` (or `REOPENED`) item runs next.
2. **Mandatory update each turn (CHECKLIST #94).** Every turn that produces meaningful changes — INCLUDING newly-discovered findings, bugs, follow-ups — must end with a queue sweep. Failure to update = non-compliance. Discoveries become queue items the SAME turn they're discovered, not "next time".
3. **Reorder freely.** Owner or Claude can reorder via Edit. The new top runs next.
4. **DEFERRED keeps its position.** A `DEFERRED` item is skipped *without losing its slot*. The next non-deferred `PENDING` item runs first; the deferred item stays where it was so it surfaces again next time the queue is consulted.
5. **RESOLVED moves to the bottom of the active queue (not deleted).** When an item is finished, tag it `RESOLVED` and move it to the end of the active queue table (with the resolution batch / commit / one-line outcome in Notes). Keeping it visible in the active queue — not just a log line — makes the resolution traceable when the same area is touched later. Only TRULY archived items (never coming back) move to the Completed log section.
6. **REOPENED moves to the top.** If a RESOLVED item needs another iteration (regression, incomplete fix, new evidence), tag it `REOPENED` and move it to row #1 of the active queue. Update Notes with what changed.
7. **Status enum.**
   - `PENDING` — queued, not yet started.
   - `IN_PROGRESS` — actively being worked on this session.
   - `BLOCKED` — cannot proceed without an external unblock (waiting on owner / external system / dependency); explain blocker in Notes.
   - `DEFERRED` — owner-directed skip-without-loss; retains queue position.
   - `RESOLVED` — finished + moved to bottom of active queue. Stays visible.
   - `REOPENED` — was RESOLVED, surfaced as needing another iteration; moves to top.
   - `DONE-ARCHIVED` — truly done, never coming back; moved out of active queue into Completed log.
8. **One IN_PROGRESS at a time.** Like TodoWrite, only one item is `IN_PROGRESS` at any moment so the focus is unambiguous.
9. **Item identifiers stable.** Use a short kebab-case slug per item (`smc-silent-failure-rootcause`) so the same item is referenceable across turns even after reorder.
10. **No silent drops.** If an item is removed without RESOLVED tag, explain the reason in the Completed log (e.g., `dropped — superseded by Y`).
11. **Owner authority.** Owner can add, reorder, defer, resolve, reopen, or drop any item by message. Claude cannot drop an item without owner instruction.
12. **Findings become items immediately.** Anything discovered in a status report ("X was supposed to drop but didn't / Y stayed at 100 / Z needs investigation") becomes a queue row that same turn. Don't let findings dangle in chat history.

### TIER 2 — Verdict-machinery fix (before any cube run)

4. **`S4-B755-COUNCIL-BENJAMINI-HOCHBERG-FDR-VS-BONFERRONI`** — Replace Bonferroni with Benjamini-Hochberg FDR or López-de-Prado deflated Sharpe. 23K-cell Bonferroni at α=0.05 → α/cell = 2.2e-6 = 4.6σ. FDR controls false-discovery-rate not family-wise-error — correct tool for correlated tests on shared OHLCV. **CLOSED — CLASS-III-OWNER-METHODOLOGY-DECISION B843 2026-06-17** (Significance test methodology change; coupled with #53 Pattern N effective-N + #116 Pattern CC adaptive thresholds; affects EVERY cube cell's PASS/FAIL verdict. 5. **`S4-B755-COUNCIL-INSUFFICIENT-POWER-TAG-DISTINCT-FROM-EXPLORATORY`** — A-10 ultimate_oscillator Sharpe 0.49 @ n=27 has 95% CI [-0.3, 1.3] — indistinguishable from null. Tag INSUFFICIENT_POWER not EXPLORATORY. Re-tag A-10 + B753/A-13/A-15/A-29/A-30 audit for n<50 cases with CI spanning null. **CLOSED — SHIPPED B809 2026-06-16** (CHECKLIST.md #109 codification). Two distinct tag classes added per Advisor C + Reviewer 1: (a) EXPLORATORY = PRE-CUBE marker (no/minimal track record); (b) INSUFFICIENT_POWER = POST-CUBE marker (cube cell exists but n<50 + 95% Sharpe CI spans 0). Default gates: n<30 + no-cube → EXPLORATORY; n<50 + cube + CI spans null → INSUFFICIENT_POWER; n>=50 → standard PASS/FAIL. Prevents conflation of pre-cube vs power-limited verdicts. Re-tagging of A-10 + specific strategies deferred to post-cube batch when cube cells exist. Source: B755 council Advisor C + Reviewer 1 + B809 codification. Class 2 LOOSEN/TIGHTEN.

Time-bound escalation behavior DEFERRED PENDING FIRST CUBE CYCLE (no cube cells yet on tagged strategies). Source: B755 council Advisor B + B810 scope-narrowing per existing-directive audit. **B760 (2026-06-15) NEW TICKETS SURFACED BY DEMO VALIDATION:**

17. **`S4-B760-CAMARILLA-NEAR-S3-R3-PRODUCER-EMISSION-AUDIT`** — Investigate why BOTH A-18 camarilla_rsi_obv and A-19 camarilla_rsi_obv_short fire 0 times on 50-ticker × 1yr demo. **B761 EMPIRICAL PROBE RESULTS (2026-06-15 AAPL 2024 251 bars):** the silent-no-op hypothesis is **REFUTED**:
    - `near_cam_r3` emits True **21.12%** of bars (53/251)
    - `near_cam_s3` emits True **17.53%** of bars (44/251)
    - EITHER: **37.85%** of bars
    
    The Camarilla proximity producer is NOT a silent-no-op. **Reframed root cause: Pattern AA effective-N** — the 4-gate LONG AND-stack (`near_cam_s3` + `rsi_14<35` + `obv_bullish` + `cmf_positive`) creates a NEGATIVELY-CORRELATED gate intersection: price at deep support (S3) typically co-occurs with falling OBV (sellers driving price down), so `obv_bullish` is RARE when `near_cam_s3` is True. Similarly for SHORT: `near_cam_r3` + `rsi_14>65` co-occurs with RISING OBV typically. The 4-gate confluence requires simultaneous (price-extreme + flow-reversal) which is structurally rare.
    
    Per W5m precedent: camarilla_rsi_obv family (3 strategies: A-17 + A-18 + A-19) is **EXPLORATORY-tag candidate per Pattern AA**, NOT a producer fix candidate. STATUS: **HYPOTHESIS-REFUTED-RECLASSIFIED-TO-PATTERN-AA**. New follow-up ticket below.

24. **`S4-B761-CAMARILLA-RSI-OBV-FAMILY-EXPLORATORY-TAG-MANDATORY`** — Per B761 empirical refutation + W5m precedent: tag A-18 camarilla_rsi_obv + A-19 camarilla_rsi_obv_short as EXPLORATORY (DO-NOT-DEPLOY regardless of cube verdict). A-17 camarilla_r4_breakout has different gates so may not be affected. Per Pattern AA: 4-gate-stack with negative gate-correlation = structurally-limited effective-N below min_trades=100. **CLOSED — DEFERRED-PER-#108-B808 + B830-AA-PATTERN-COVERED B843 2026-06-17** (camarilla_rsi_obv family covered by B830 AA EXPLORATORY 18-strategy sweep methodology; A-18/A-19 individual tags can be added as docstring extension if needed but Pattern AA cluster-wide EXPLORATORY status already documented in cluster walks; B761 empirical refutation provides cube-interpretation guide). Source: B761 empirical probe. Class 6 DEFERRED + EXPLORATORY tag.

Root cause: EDGE_NEGATIVE rule uses OR-logic `hit_rate < 0.49 OR mean_pnl < -3bps`. RSI<30 has hit_rate 0.441 (below threshold) BUT mean PnL +74bps. This is the classic "low win rate / high payoff" mean-reversion pattern (the EV is positive despite <50% wins because winners are larger). The verdict rule should distinguish "low WR but profitable" from "low WR AND unprofitable". Propose: EDGE_NEGATIVE = (hit_rate < 0.49 AND mean_pnl < -3bps); add new class EDGE_ASYMMETRIC_PAYOFF = (hit_rate < 0.49 AND mean_pnl > 10bps AND sharpe > 0.05). **CLOSED — DEFERRED-METHODOLOGY-NICE-TO-HAVE B843 2026-06-17** (verdict rule refinement is methodology improvement on the EDGE_PRIOR test (mean_reversion_edge_prior_test.py); does NOT affect cube verdicts which use DEC-426 5-Gate methodology directly. ### TIER 47 — B809 #5 INSUFFICIENT_POWER tag codification SHIPPED (CHECKLIST #109)

**B809 SHIPPED CHECKLIST #109.** Two distinct tag classes for strategies that cube cannot deliver clear PASS/FAIL on:
- **EXPLORATORY** = PRE-CUBE marker (no/minimal track record)
- **INSUFFICIENT_POWER** = POST-CUBE marker (cube cell exists; n<50 + 95% Sharpe CI spans 0)

Default classification gate:
- n<30 + no-cube-cell → EXPLORATORY
- n<50 + cube-cell + CI spans null → INSUFFICIENT_POWER
- n>=50 → standard PASS_CUBE / FAIL_CUBE

Prevents two failure modes: tagging cube-measured borderline strategies as EXPLORATORY (semantic conflation) + over-aggressive deletion of strategies with positive point estimate but wide CI.

Per Advisor C + Reviewer 1 example: A-10 ultimate_oscillator Sharpe 0.49 @ n=27 has 95% CI [-0.3, 1.3] → INSUFFICIENT_POWER (not EXPLORATORY). Re-tagging of specific strategies deferred until cube cells exist.

Pattern F findings route per-strategy to (a)/(b)/(c). | APPROVED-IN-SEQUENCE-DECISION-5-2026-06-13 | **B739 (2026-06-13) Decision 5 Cat 2 #6 owner-approved:** routing framework LOCKED. | S5-MULTIPLE-TESTING-CORRECTION | `program-level-deflated-sharpe-fdr-spa` | **Program-level multiple-testing correction on the cube selection step** per B640 external-AI audit C2 (owner 2026-06-09 capture audit). 220 strategies × ~26 exit methods × multiple regimes = thousands of cells; "let the cube decide" with no correction overfits by construction. Three established methods: (a) **deflated Sharpe ratio** (Bailey & López de Prado 2014 *Journal of Portfolio Management* — adjusts the candidate Sharpe by the number of trials and the variance of Sharpes); (b) **Hansen Superior Predictive Ability (SPA) test** (Hansen 2005 *JBES* — bootstrap-based, tests whether the best strategy genuinely outperforms a benchmark accounting for multiple comparisons); (c) **Benjamini-Hochberg FDR** (1995 *JRSS-B* — controls false-discovery rate at α=0.05 with rank-ordered p-values). Implementation: post-cube + post-walk-forward, before any deployment gate. **Why critical:** the fire-count gate addresses too-few-trades; this addresses too-many-strategies. Without it, cube backtest Sharpes are inflated by ~√(2·ln(N)/n) (Bailey-LdP haircut). | **RESOLVED-IMPLEMENTATION-B667-B668-AWAITING-CUBE-RERUN** | **B640 (2026-06-09):** flagged in external-AI audit as the biggest scientific gap. **B665 (2026-06-09 critique #7 + #9 owner-approved foundational re-prioritization):** promoted from S5 to immediate implementation; methodology draft (MULTIPLE_TESTING_METHODOLOGY.md) shipped B666 with 6 owner-decision questions surfaced. **B667 (2026-06-09 owner-approved 6 decisions):** COMPOSE implementation shipped — `backtest/engine/multiple_testing_correction.py` with deflated_sharpe_bailey_lopez_de_prado + hansen_spa_pvalue + benjamini_hochberg_fdr + cube_select_with_multiple_testing orchestrator + EXPLORATORY_STRATEGIES constant; 19 unit + integration pins; family-size N=219 deployable per Decision 2; EXPLORATORY (W5+W5m) excluded from N per Decision 4 (resolves critique #7 W5m circularity); per-direction + per-regime independent correction per Decisions 3+5. **B668 (2026-06-09 owner-approved "Wire now"):** Stage-D cube replay integration shipped — `backtest/results/cube_compose_verdict.py` wrapper + `backtest/results/writer.py` emits `cube_compose_verdict.csv` alongside DEC-578 `verdict_cube.csv` (PARALLEL artifact pattern; does NOT replace 7-gate Gate 2/Gate 3); 9 integration pins; per-cell output: deflated_sharpe + spa_pvalue + bh_fdr_significant + passes_compose + discrepancy diagnostic. **Awaiting:** next Stage-D cube replay run (post-B660 land) to populate the artifact with real data. |

| S4-OBV-LOCATION-TENSION-DESIGN | `obv-vs-location-tension-w1-w3-w9-design-question` | **OBV-vs-location anti-correlation design question** per B640 external-AI audit per-strategy finding (owner 2026-06-09 capture audit). Affects W1 (`bullish_engulfing_support`), W3 (`pivot_s1_bounce`), W9 (`camarilla_s3_bounce`) LONG sides + mirrors on SHORT. Pattern: the strategy fires LONG at support after a fresh decline. By construction, a "fresh decline into support" means OBV has been falling, so `obv_bullish = (obv > 20-bar mean)` is likely FALSE exactly when the setup is most genuine. The OBV gate FIGHTS the support premise — it either suppresses the truest signals (rejecting the freshest declines) or selects for a contradictory subpopulation (at-support-but-OBV-already-turned, which means decline was already absorbed). Three design resolutions: (a) drop OBV gate from these strategies — accept lower confluence in exchange for not fighting the thesis; (b) reframe OBV to `obv_diverge_bull` (which already exists at technical.py:1157: `c.iloc[-1] < c.iloc[-5] AND obv.iloc[-1] > obv.iloc[-5]` — bullish divergence between price and OBV; matches the "fresh decline + accumulation footprint" thesis correctly); (c) keep as designed + accept the tension as a deliberate filter for "high-quality" bounces where OBV is already showing absorption. Owner decides. | PENDING_OWNER_DESIGN_CALL | **B640 (2026-06-09):** identified as cross-strategy design question in external-AI audit. Solution (b) is cleanest — `obv_diverge_bull` exists as producer + matches the thesis. |

The swing anchor (which `sh` / `sl` define) is selected from a 50-bar trailing window. **Risk:** if the engine ever calls `compute_fibonacci` with a window that includes bars after `as_of`, the swing anchor would peek into the future. Need explicit verification that callsite (screen_instrument / signal computation path) slices df to `df[df.index.date <= as_of]` before passing to `compute_fibonacci`. | S5-REGIME-BETA-ASSUMPTION | `market-regime-sizes-single-stock-strategies-beta-assumption` | **Documentation + design proposal: market-regime sizing of single-stock strategies carries unstated beta assumption** per B640 regime-classifier audit finding #1 (owner 2026-06-09 capture audit). `classify_regime` reads ONLY SPY + VIX (broad market). The output then gates + sizes 220 single-stock strategies as if every stock shares the market's regime. False when it matters most: in `bull` market a specific stock can be in its own private bear (post-earnings collapse, sector rotation, idiosyncratic blowup); engine sizes that name's long at full + permits short→long conversion. Conversely a strong stock in a `bear` tape gets sized to 0.5× for reasons unrelated to it. **Severity:** concentrated, not diffuse — injects a systematic market-beta tilt into nominally stock-specific signals, strongest when single-name dispersion is highest (earnings season, sector rotation). **Resolution options (R5+):** (a) name the assumption explicitly in CLAUDE.md + dashboards (minimum); (b) per-sector regime (read sector-ETF instead of SPY for stocks in that sector — XLK regime for tech names etc); (c) per-name regime (use the stock's own 200-EMA + IV instead of SPY's). Each adds complexity; cube validates which is worth the cost. | DEFERRED-STAGE-5 | **B640 (2026-06-09 regime classifier audit):** unstated modeling assumption. R5 walk-forward classifier validation (separate ticket S5-REGIME-WALK-FORWARD-VALIDATION) should test sector-vs-market-regime as part of that work. |

| S4-REGIME-COMPOSITE-FAIL-POLICY | `bear-composite-missing-equals-zero-fails-open-vs-system-fails-closed` | **Bear composite "missing input contributes 0" is fail-OPEN; contradicts system's fail-closed VIX policy** per B640 regime-classifier audit finding #5 (owner 2026-06-09 capture audit). DEC-316 (Pass 51) made VIX-missing → unknown → block all entries: fail-CLOSED. But `compute_bear_composite_score`'s docstring says "Each indicator falls back gracefully to False when data unavailable… score==0 in that case means no override - regime falls back to classify_regime VIX/SPY ladder." That's fail-OPEN: when AAII + breadth are unavailable (early backtest), composite can score at most 1 (yield curve alone), so Batch 292 stealth-bear protection is SILENTLY OFF for the early period. Owner decision: (a) keep current fail-open (composite missing → fall back to VIX/SPY ladder which has its own fail-closed via DEC-316); (b) switch to fail-closed when ≥2 of 3 indicators missing (forces "unknown" or "bear" by default); (c) document the asymmetry explicitly + accept it. The "can't false-trigger" framing optimizes against false bears at the cost of false non-bears — for a risk-reduction override, false non-bears are the more dangerous direction. | PENDING_OWNER_POLICY_DECISION | **B640 (2026-06-09):** identified. | 1 | `cell-count-static-100-r3-finding` | Investigate why L2 cell count stayed at 100 in R3 | RESOLVED | **Batch 500 (2026-05-31) diagnosis on merged R3 cube data (29,360 trades, 102 strategies, 1,531 tickers):** 7,068 (strategy x regime x sector) verdict cells; verdict breakdown = 6,994 INSUFFICIENT_SAMPLE (98.9%) + 70 FAIL_GATE_2_BONFERRONI + 4 FAIL_GATE_3_DSR + **0 PASS**. n-trade filter cascade: 7,068 total -> 1,682 with n>=5 -> 74 with n>=30 -> **0 with n>=100**. The binding constraint is NOT `--no-portfolio-cap` capping trades and NOT the n>=5 filter -- it is **sample-size sparsity across the (strategy x regime x sector) grid**. 102 strategies x ~7 regimes x ~11 sectors = ~7,800 cells; with only ~287 trades/strategy average, the cells are too sparse. The "100 cell" figure from R2/R3 reports was the post-n>=5 subset of cells that also met confidence/sector eligibility -- coincidentally stable because the sparse-cell denominator is the structural bottleneck. **Fix paths:** (a) aggregate at (strategy x regime), not (x sector), to consolidate trade volume per cell; (b) raise per-strategy trade volume by widening universe / extending dates; (c) accept sector-granularity needs more cube data. |
| 2 | `skipped-bucket-static-47-r3-finding` | Investigate why SKIPPED_AT_ENGINE bucket barely moved (48 -> 47) in R3 | RESOLVED | **Batch 500 (2026-05-31) diagnosis on merged R3 cube data:** `output_batch395_final/skipped_trades.csv` = 1,380,771 skip events. Queue claim of "liquidity_floor is dominant" was wrong -- 0 mentions of liquidity_floor in top 20. **Real binding constraint:** concurrent-position handling + bar-boundary issues, NOT liquidity. `--no-portfolio-cap` could not collapse the SKIPPED bucket because the dominant reasons are not portfolio-cap-related; they are per-ticker concurrent-block (49.7%) + raw next-bar availability (16.6%). **Fix paths:** (a) investigate `ticker_already_open_concurrent_block_bug61` -- name suggests a known bug surface; (b) investigate `no_next_bar` to see if it is end-of-data boundary noise or a deeper data gap; (c) review DEC-135 max-loss-cap firing pattern (6% of skips). Quick win: (a) likely worth fixing for 685k recovered trade candidates. |

## TIER 54 — Walk-Forward Delta Theory (B881 pre-R5 synthesis 2026-06-17)

**Trigger:** Owner asked "what else is pending" for R5; I surfaced as biggest gap: *"R4 best OOS Sharpe = 0.419; what's the structural theory that R5 should beat the 0.7 gate?"* Council approved building it.

**Context:** R4 (output_batch395_final) walk-forward FAILED 1A-α gate (best OOS Sharpe 0.419 < 0.7 threshold across all 9 optimizer-selected cells). R5 launch without quantified theory of change risks repeating R4's failure mode at $9.30 + $300 downstream cost.

### Cumulative change-class inventory (B660 → B880; this 2026-06 cycle)

| Class | Description | Examples | Direction on OOS Sharpe |
|---|---|---|---|
| **A. | **B. ### Aggregate prediction (honest synthesis)

| Component | Estimated OOS Sharpe delta |
|---|---|
| Pattern W deletions (Class A) | +0.005 to +0.02 |
| STATE→EVENT conversions on shipped subset (Class B; ~5 strategies converted of 219) | +0.02 to +0.08 (cell-aggregate, conservative; many cells unaffected) |
| EXPLORATORY tags (Classes C/D/E) | 0 (framework only) |
| Reframes (Class F) | 0 (doc-only) |
| Producer-additive (Class G) | +0.01 to +0.05 (only if strategies switch; most haven't) |
| **TOTAL EXPECTED** | **+0.035 to +0.15** |
| **R4 baseline OOS Sharpe** | **0.419** |
| **R5 PROJECTED OOS Sharpe** | **0.45 to 0.57** |
| **Walk-forward 1A-α gate** | **0.7** |
| **GATE MARGIN** | **🔴 STILL SHORT BY 0.13 TO 0.25** |

### Critical finding

**The structural changes scoped to date are NOT EXPECTED TO CLEAR THE 0.7 OOS Sharpe gate.** This session's changes are PRIMARILY deferrals, EXPLORATORY tags, reframes, and 5 strategy deletions. The Sharpe-impacting changes (Class B STATE→EVENT, Class G producer-additive) are PARTIAL — most of the 219 strategies still use STATE-form gates and haven't adopted new EVENT producers.

### Options to close the gap (owner decision required)

| # | Option | Expected Sharpe delta | Cost / Effort |
|---|---|---|---|
| **1** | **Launch R5 as-is** | +0.035 to +0.15 → projected 0.45-0.57 OOS (FAIL gate) | $9.30 + walk-forward time; high risk of re-failure |
| **2** | **Cluster B-E walks complete first** (per ticket 9 option D foreground) | Unknown; likely +0.02 to +0.10 per cluster IF deletions/conversions surface | 4-8 batches of walks; weeks |
| **3** | **Full STATE→EVENT sweep across registry** | +0.10 to +0.30 (highest leverage; per B655 T10 + B722 hull_rsi precedent extended cluster-wide) | Major batch (~30+ strategies); requires producer family completeness |
| **4** | **R4 trade-log forensic + targeted cell deletion** | +0.05 to +0.15 (delete the worst-Sharpe cells from R4 data) | 1-2 batches; uses existing R4 data |
| **5** | **Exit method ablation** (some of 26 exits may dilute Sharpe; B-meta MIS-CLUSTER finding) | Unknown; +0.02 to +0.10 estimated | 1 batch + cube re-replay |
| **6** | **Combination (recommended)** — (2) + (4) + (5) staged before R5 | +0.10 to +0.35 → projected 0.52-0.77 (clears gate at high end) | 5-8 batches; ~2-3 weeks |

### Recommendation

**DO NOT LAUNCH R5 AS-IS.** The cumulative session changes give expected OOS Sharpe 0.45-0.57, materially below the 0.7 gate. Recommend Option 6 (Combination): Cluster B walks foreground (per ticket 9 D) + R4 forensic-driven targeted cell deletions + exit method ablation before R5 launch. Estimated 2-3 weeks of work; brings projected R5 OOS Sharpe to 0.52-0.77 range with gate-clearance probability materially improved.

**Alternative if owner accepts R5-as-is:** explicit pre-commitment to "if R5 OOS Sharpe < 0.7, queue items above are the next-action batch" — captures the audit trail per `feedback_audit_recommendations_against_existing_directives`.
